# Skills · L · part 66

843 entries, lipid-class-abundance-profiling to lisa-agent-design-best-practices. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lipid-class-abundance-profiling**: Use when profiling lipidomics abundance data and you need sample-level or group-level composition summaries at the lipid-class level rather than per lipid species, e.g. to compare class percentages across samples in a multi-group dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-abundance-profiling))
- **lipid-class-adduct-ccs-regression-correction**: Use when LC-IM-MS lipidomics samples are spiked with fully U13C-labeled yeast extract lipids, measured CCS values exist per lipid class/adduct, and TIMCCSN2-vs-DTCCSN2 bias needs correction via lipid class- and adduct-specific linear regression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-adduct-ccs-regression-correction))
- **lipid-class-adduct-enumeration**: Use when building a reference in-silico lipid fragmentation library for LC-MS/MS spectral matching, or when verifying locally generated msp lipid libraries against the deposited MTBLS3097 reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-adduct-enumeration))
- **lipid-class-adduct-filtering**: Use when a measured IM-MS lipidomics table (File, LipidClass, LipidSpecies, Adduct, Label, CCS) must be matched against the U13C_DT_CCS_library.csv DTCCSN2 library and invalid or nomenclature-mismatched class-adduct pairs must be excluded before CCS bias calculation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-adduct-filtering))
- **lipid-class-adduct-parameter-selection**: Use when building an in silico lipid spectral library with LSG and you must decide which lipid class(es), fatty-acid compositions, and adduct(s) to include for LC-MS/MS identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-adduct-parameter-selection))
- **lipid-class-adduct-subset-resampling**: Use when measured IM-MS lipidomics data (.csv with File, LipidClass, LipidSpecies, Adduct, Label, CCS columns) contains heavy (U13C-labeled) lipids and CCSN2 values need class- and adduct-specific correction against a DTCCSN2 reference library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-adduct-subset-resampling))
- **lipid-class-aggregation-from-molecular-species**: Use when the input is a molecular-species-level lipidomic data matrix (rows = lipid species in LIPID MAPS shorthand notation, columns = samples) and a lipid-class-level (LCL) matrix is needed for downstream class-level statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-aggregation-from-molecular-species))
- **lipid-class-and-fatty-acid-enumeration**: Use when an LC-MS/MS lipid identification workflow needs a custom, on-demand in silico spectral library covering specified lipid classes, fatty acid compositions, and adducts, rather than a fixed pre-built library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-and-fatty-acid-enumeration))
- **lipid-class-annotation-against-metlin-hmdb**: Use when a peak-aligned, TIC-normalized SCMS feature table from two cell phenotypes (e.g., primary vs metastatic melanoma, ± drug treatment) needs differential lipid species filtered at fold change ≥ 2.0 and FDR-adjusted p ≤ 0.05 and annotated to lipid classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-annotation-against-metlin-hmdb))
- **lipid-class-annotation-and-parsing**: Use when immediately after loading raw lipid identifiers from LipidSearch or LIQUID output, or when importing lipidomic data from the Metabolomics Workbench. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-class-annotation-and-parsing))
- **lipid-class-annotation-from-names**: Use when lipid feature identifiers are name strings (e.g. from a Skyline export or Metabolomics Workbench study like ST001111) and you need class/chain/unsaturation annotations, including renaming full-chemical-name ceramides to shorthand. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-annotation-from-names))
- **lipid-class-annotation-level-matching**: Use when a per-lipid LipidLynxX Linker cross-reference table must be scored for identifier coverage against integration databases, and class-level gaps (e.g. which of the 131 lipids lack LION/KEGG/ChEBI IDs) need to be summarized. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-annotation-level-matching))
- **lipid-class-annotation-visualization**: Use when you have lsea enrichment results (e.g., ranked by logFC from a two-group lipidomics contrast) and need to render class-level or chain-length enrichment plots. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-annotation-visualization))
- **lipid-class-chain-annotation-inference**: Use when importing lipidomics matrices, Skyline exports, or Metabolomics Workbench studies into lipidr and you need lipid class/chain annotations parsed from molecule names, or need to detect unparseable names. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-chain-annotation-inference))
- **lipid-class-coverage-assessment**: Use when when you have acquired a CCS reference library (such as DTCCSN2 for U13C labeled lipids) and need to verify that it contains the expected lipid classes, CCS values are physically plausible for ion mobility data, and coverage matches the library's advertised documentation before using it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-class-coverage-assessment) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-class-enrichment-interpretation**: Use when interpreting S2IsoMEr bootstrapping ORA/MSEA output tables of lipid-class terms (HMDB sub_class, LION) from MS1 imaging or single-cell metabolomics comparisons of conditions, e.g. fatty-acid-treated vs untreated HepaRG cells. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-enrichment-interpretation))
- **lipid-class-enrichment-visualization**: Use when a differential-expression table of lipid species (with p-values) is available and the goal is to identify which lipid classes or lipid sets are enriched among up- or down-regulated species, and to visualize those enrichments per class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-enrichment-visualization))
- **lipid-class-enumeration**: Use when you have a lipid identification or library-generation task that requires you to define a target chemical space bounded by lipid classes (e.g., phosphatidylcholine, triglyceride) and fatty acid composition ranges (e.g., C14–C22 with 0–6 degrees of unsaturation). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-class-enumeration) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-class-feature-annotation**: Use when when loading lipidomics data (from Skyline CSV, numerical matrix, or Metabolomics Workbench) and encountering parsing warnings or molecules with names that do not follow standard lipid nomenclature patterns (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-class-feature-annotation))
- **lipid-class-frequency-thresholding**: Use when compiling a reference MS/MS training library from a public spectral database (e.g. LipidMaps-derived .msp blocks) and you must decide which compound classes have enough spectra to support a data-centric model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-frequency-thresholding))
- **lipid-class-grouped-linear-modeling**: Use when you have a LipiDex 2 pipeline peak table with lipid identifications, retention times, and fatty acyl compositions, and need a class-aware RT QC model to flag likely incorrect identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-grouped-linear-modeling))
- **lipid-class-library-composition-analysis**: Use when comparing lipid-class/adduct coverage across in-silico lipidomics libraries (MAVEN2, MS-DIAL, CalicoLipids) to find classes present in only one library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-library-composition-analysis))
- **lipid-class-matched-ccs-model-training**: Use when a metabolomics annotation workflow needs CCS-based false-positive filtering for lipid/lipid-like [M-H]- candidates, requiring a training set restricted to the same molecular superclass as the targets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-matched-ccs-model-training))
- **lipid-class-over-representation-analysis**: Use when a differential expression result table (e.g., the deSp_multiGroup output from a multi-group lipidomics analysis) contains significant lipid species that should be tested for over-representation in lipid characteristics such as lipid class. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-over-representation-analysis))
- **lipid-class-prefix-parsing**: Use when a feature table of lipid identifications (e.g., Additional file 3 Table S4 LipidMatch annotations) lacks an explicit class column and you need distinct lipid ion, feature, or lipid-type counts to verify reported numbers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-prefix-parsing))
- **lipid-class-regulation-pattern-interpretation**: Use when a lipidomics dataset with sample group annotations (e.g. cancer vs benign, cancer vs metastasis) has been differential-analyzed with lipidr and you need to summarize which lipid classes show consistent regulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-regulation-pattern-interpretation))
- **lipid-class-stratified-analysis**: Use when you have IM-MS lipidomics data with measured CCS values, samples spiked with U13C-labeled lipid internal standards (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-class-stratified-analysis) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-class-stratified-benchmarking**: Use when you have predicted molecular fingerprints for a set of extracted MS/MS spectra and need to quantify annotation accuracy overall and per chemical class (e.g., lipid classes) against a reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-stratified-benchmarking))
- **lipid-class-subclass-classification**: Use when an annotated m/z feature table (e.g. an all_IsomerNames-style metadata column from a spatial metabolomics experiment) holds multi-isomer lipid annotation strings that need standardising into common lipid categories and classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-subclass-classification))
- **lipid-class-subnetwork-interpretation**: Use when MS/MS networking of differential metabolomics features (e.g., &gt;1000 enriched features) must be resolved into lipid-class subnetworks such as N-acyl ethanolamide families for biological interpretation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-subnetwork-interpretation))
- **lipid-class-template-construction**: Use when a discovery lipidomics study targets lipid classes not in a prebuilt database such as LipidBlast or Lipid Maps — e.g. glycolipids, lipopeptides, sphingomyelin, FAHFA, ornithine lipids — or requires fragmentation patterns for unique MSn methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-class-template-construction))
- **lipid-common-name-decoding**: Use when input lipid identifiers are common/RefMet names (e.g. 'octadecatrienoic acid', 'linolenic acid', 'linoleyl palmitate') rather than CLASS N:N/M:M shorthand, and you need carbon:double-bond residues. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-common-name-decoding))
- **lipid-component-accuracy-metric-computation**: Use when evaluating LipiDetective transformer predictions on labeled test MS2 spectra and you need a finer-grained accuracy metric than whole-species match rate, e.g. to show performance when full species predictions are partly wrong. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-component-accuracy-metric-computation))
- **lipid-compound-class-annotation-from-spectral-matches**: Use when MS2Query (or a similar library-matching tool) has returned per-spectrum matches for complex-mixture metabolomics data and you must check whether annotations agree with the expected compound class (e.g., lipids in plasma). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-compound-class-annotation-from-spectral-matches))
- **lipid-concentration-normalization**: Use when your lipidomics experiment includes spiked internal lipid standards with known absolute concentrations, and you have a data matrix of signal intensities (samples × lipids) from LipidSearch or LIQUID output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-concentration-normalization) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-convert**: Convert lipid shorthand notation to molecular structures (SMILES, InChI, InChIKey, MOL files, SDF files). Use when the user asks about lipid structures, needs to look up a lipid by name, wants SMILES or InChIKey for database searches, or needs structure files for visualization or cheminformatics tools. ([MontgomeryBohde/PyLipidParse](https://github.com/MontgomeryBohde/PyLipidParse/tree/HEAD/claude-code-plugin/skills/lipid-convert) · ★21)
- **lipid-correlation-network-reconstruction**: Use when you have a targeted-lipidomics abundance matrix (lipid species such as PI, PC, PE, PS, PG and AA-containing species as rows; e.g. 0.01/1 U/mL thrombin, 1/5 µg/mL CRP plus unstimulated control as columns) and need to test class-level depletion claims and co-regulation structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-correlation-network-reconstruction))
- **lipid-coverage-assessment**: Use when after hierarchical fragmentation library matching has produced candidate lipid annotations for a multi-species LC-MS/MS dataset, and you have applied retention time–based filtering rules (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-coverage-assessment) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-database-accession-linking**: Use when lipid shorthand annotations (e.g. PC 16:0_18:2, MS-DIAL/LipidSearch-style abbreviations, DHA-style aliases) must be cross-matched to LIPID MAPS, SwissLipids, or PubChem accession IDs for cross-database comparison or systems biology integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-database-accession-linking))
- **lipid-database-query-and-annotation**: Use when you have parsed MRM transition data (m/z values, retention times, transition parameters) from mass spectrometry experiments and need to map each detected transition to a known lipid identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-database-query-and-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-database-querying**: Use when you have acquired full-scan mass spectrometry imaging data (e.g., from a mouse bladder or tissue section) with detected m/z features and want to assign chemical identities to those features by querying a structured lipid database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-database-querying) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-database-structure-retrieval**: Use when a structural-space or structural-similarity analysis needs molecular graphs for named lipid species (e.g., PC 12:0/[12-24,26]:0 series) and only species names/SMILES-free identifiers are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-database-structure-retrieval))
- **lipid-derivatization-chemistry-modeling**: Use when you have N-methyl-derivatized unsaturated sterol lipid structures (as SMILES or molecular formula) and need to predict their MS/MS fragmentation behavior before experimental acquisition, or to build a reference spectral library for isomer-level sterol identification in tissue samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-derivatization-chemistry-modeling) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-double-bond-position-extraction**: Use when lipid structure records carry explicit double bond positions on fatty acyl chains and you must quantify how often two arbitrary chains share no double bond at the same position under forward (carbonyl) and backward (omega) numbering, or need a reference-database ground truth for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-double-bond-position-extraction))
- **lipid-expression-pattern-visualization**: Use when after statistical analysis of lipid abundance data has produced a table of lipid identities, expression measurements, p-values, fold-changes, and condition labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-expression-pattern-visualization) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-feature-abundance-comparison**: Use when LC/MS feature tables from two or more biological groups (e.g., WT vs hacl-1 mutant on OP50 vs cyclopropane-deficient JW1653-1 diets) must be compared to find features enriched in one genotype that additionally depend on the bacterial diet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-abundance-comparison))
- **lipid-feature-annotation-against-lipidpioneer**: Use when lipid features from traveling-wave IM-MS (m/z, charge, retention time, arrival time) have been class-assigned and CCS-calibrated and you need database-backed validation of exact mass and CCS against curated lipid reference values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-annotation-against-lipidpioneer))
- **lipid-feature-annotation-and-sorting**: Use when after quantifying ion images in LipidQMap and before exporting to HDF5 format, when you need to organize per-feature metadata (lipid ID, class, adduct, m/z, internal standard flag) into aligned datasets that can be linked to intensity data via dimension scales and sorted for reproducible. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-feature-annotation-and-sorting) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-feature-count-reconciliation**: Use when a LipiDex 2 (or similar) discovery lipidomics run has Peak Finder and Spectral Searcher outputs plus Degreaser QC filtering results, and you must report how feature counts and identification rate changed after filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-count-reconciliation))
- **lipid-feature-identifier-matching**: Use when you have an isotope-corrected or raw MSI dataset stored in HDF5 format following Cardinal::HDF5 conventions, a user-provided internal standard definition (sample identifier and/or feature name), and need to locate and extract the intensity row for that lipid before performing ratio-based. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-feature-identifier-matching) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-feature-importance-ranking**: Use when you have MALDI MSI peak data co-registered with an IFM segmentation mask (e.g. thresholded Ly6G marker) and need to rank which lipids (m/z features) classify marker-positive vs marker-negative pixels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-importance-ranking))
- **lipid-feature-importance-selection**: Use when LipidIN-annotated lipid feature tables from patient cohorts (e.g., breast cancer vs. healthy plasma LC-MS lipidomics, Supplementary Data 9) are available and a small diagnostic panel with cross-cohort generalization is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-importance-selection))
- **lipid-feature-normalization**: Use when after raw lipidomic and metabolomic data files have been generated by the Multi-ABLE method and loaded into the R environment, but before performing multivariate statistical analysis to identify differential lipids and metabolites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-feature-normalization) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-feature-normalization**: Use when a lipid feature/abundance matrix plus a phenotype file must be normalized (mstus) and decomposed via lipidr into lipid class, chain length, and saturation features before differential lipid analysis in MOI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-normalization))
- **lipid-feature-one-hot-encoding**: Use when training or applying lipid CCS prediction models that need tabular lipid measurements (lipid class, fatty acid sum composition, adduct, m/z) converted to a fixed-width numeric feature matrix for scikit-learn estimators. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-one-hot-encoding))
- **lipid-feature-qc-filtering**: Use when a lipidomics peak table (e.g. from Compound Discoverer 3.3 .cdResult) contains adduct peaks, dimers, or identifications failing QC evidence such as &gt;30% RSD in QC samples, and must be filtered before quantitation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-qc-filtering))
- **lipid-feature-quality-control**: Use when raw peak lists from MSI sections or single-cell SCMS runs contain mass-shifted duplicate m/z peaks, and you need to retain only mutually observed, low-occurrence-filtered lipid features for downstream annotation or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-quality-control))
- **lipid-feature-sequential-forward-selection**: Use when a lipidomics abundance table is annotated with sample-associated study variables (e.g., BMI, tissue, condition) and you need the lipid subset that distinguishes each variable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-feature-sequential-forward-selection))
- **lipid-fingerprint-regeneration**: Use when MS2 library search (EQ) plus LCI filtering leaves low-confidence or unannotated LC-MS/MS lipid features that need recall boosting via regenerated fingerprint spectra from a pre-trained deep-learning model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-fingerprint-regeneration))
- **lipid-fingerprint-regeneration-neural-networks**: Use when you have MS/MS spectra with initial lipid annotations from spectral library matching (e.g., from XCMS + CAMERA or LipidIN's Expeditious Querying module) and seek to improve recall, precision, and annotation coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-fingerprint-regeneration-neural-networks) · 4 copies)
- **lipid-fluorescence-correlation-in-steatosis-model**: Use when a per-cell SpaceM matrix (MORPHnMOL.csv) containing normalized lipid intensities plus microscopy-derived fluorescence such as LD540 must be screened for lipids that positively or negatively mark a steatotic (lipid-droplet-rich) cell subpopulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-fluorescence-correlation-in-steatosis-model))
- **lipid-fragment-annotation-matching**: Use when you have per-peak attribution scores from a deep learning lipidomics model and need to verify that top-attributed m/z values correspond to known, interpretable lipid fragments rather than spurious spectral features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-fragment-annotation-matching))
- **lipid-fragment-intensity-ranking**: Use when a single feature (defined by m/z and retention time) has multiple co-eluting lipid candidates with similar m/z values and you must decide which identification to report as the primary annotation. - **lipid-fragment-library-matching**: Use when annotating lipid features from all-ion fragmentation (AIF) LC–MS lipidomics datasets (e.g., human serum Lipid+ or Lipid− modes) that require parent–fragment matching against curated lipid fragment libraries rather than generic MS/MS spectral search. - **lipid-fragment-mass-probability-computation**: Use when designing or auditing a targeted lipidomics PRM/MRM assay: you must decide how many MS/MS fragments (and which tolerance, e.g. ±0.5 Da vs ±2.5 ppm) are needed so that target lipids cannot be confused with decoys within the sample lipidome background. - **lipid-fragment-mz-matching**: Use when feature tables paired with .ms2 tandem MS files are available and lipid identifications must be assigned by matching observed fragment m/z values to in silico library fragments of candidate lipids from MS1 exact-mass matching. - **lipid-fragmentation-pattern-application**: Use when you have an enumerated list of lipid species (identified by class, fatty acid composition, and chain length) and need to generate theoretical precursor m/z values, fragment ion masses, and relative intensities for targeted or untargeted lipidomics experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-fragmentation-pattern-application) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-fragmentation-pattern-prediction**: Use when you have defined lipid species (class, chain composition, and adducts) and need to generate precursor–fragment transition pairs for targeted lipidomics experiments. - **lipid-fragmentation-pattern-recognition**: Use when you have experimental tandem MS (MS/MS) spectra from lipid samples (in mzML format) and need to assign molecular identities and lipid classes. - **lipid-graph-representation**: Use when when you have multiple lipid structures (from lipidomics data in CSV, XLSX, or mzTab-M format) and need to compute pairwise structural distances, identify lipids responsible for shaping a lipidome, or perform hierarchical clustering of lipidomes. - **lipid-identification-confidence-tiering**: Use when a peak-picked, aligned HILIC-IM-MS feature table must be annotated with lipid identifications whose confidence is graded by how many dimensions (m/z, retention time, CCS) agree within defined tolerances. - **lipid-identification-filter-status-validation**: Use when LipiDex 2 Degreaser automated threshold filtering has flagged lipid identifications for removal and a mass spectrometrist must manually validate those statuses via QC plots, or when reproducing a manual-validation QC count against reported totals. - **lipid-identification-level-assignment**: Use when downstream interpretation needs to distinguish how structurally resolved a lipid annotation is — e.g. separating class-level IDs from fatty-acyl-constituent IDs after fragment matching. - **lipid-identification-quality-filtering**: Use when you have MS-DIAL lipid identification results (alignment exports in msp/txt format) and need to distinguish correct from incorrect lipid IDs before downstream analysis. - **lipid-identification-scoring**: Use when after peak picking has generated a peaklist of experimental fragment m/z values (from Q-Exactive orbitrap, Agilent/Bruker/SCIEX Q-TOF UHPLC-HRMS/MS, or direct infusion/imaging experiments) and you need to compare those fragments against the LipidMatch in-silico fragmentation library. - **lipid-identifier-cross-mapping**: Use when lipid annotation lists from tools like MS-DIAL, LipidSearch, or LipidBlast must be converted to a common identifier scheme or cross-mapped to LIPID MAPS, SwissLipids, PubChem, KEGG, ChEBI, or LION IDs for dataset compilation or meta-analysis. - **lipid-identifier-cross-matching**: Use when two or more lipidomics annotation lists report lipids at different structural annotation levels (e.g. PC 16:0/18:2 vs PC 16:0_18:2 vs PC 34:2) and they must be compiled or compared at a shared level. - **lipid-identifier-parsing**: Use when a processed lipidomics table (lipid identifiers as vertical index, samples as columns) needs its analyte names decomposed into fatty acid residues such as 18:1 or 20:4, whether names follow the generic 'CLASS N:N/M:M' form, isomer-group form, or RefMet nomenclature. - **lipid-isomer-ambiguity-handling**: Use when lipid identifiers contain unresolved mass-isomer groups such as 'TAG 48:2 total (14:0/16:0/18:2)(14:0/16:1/18:1)(16:0/16:1/16:1)', and residue extraction must account for or discard isobaric uncertainty. - **lipid-library-curation**: Use when you have obtained MobiLipid or a similar IM-MS lipidomics package that bundles a CCS reference library for labeled lipids, and you need to verify library integrity, validate that all expected lipid species are present with plausible numeric CCS values, and prepare a canonical curated. - **lipid-precursor-mass-computation**: Use when designing targeted lipidomics PRM/MRM assays: you need to compute monoisotopic precursor m/z and diagnostic fragment masses from lipid class definitions, fatty acyl chains, and adducts, or generate transition lists for Skyline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-precursor-mass-computation))
- **lipid-quantification-qc-curve-validation**: Use when a LipidCreator-generated transition list has been measured as PRM/SRM triplicate LC-MS/MS injections (e.g., 5 µL, Q-Exactive HF or QTRAP 6500) and you must quantify targeted plasma lipids, filter species by curve linearity and precision, and compare to NIST SRM 1950. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-quantification-qc-curve-validation))
- **lipid-refmet-analyte-class-mapping**: Use when a user-supplied analyte list or uploaded measurement table must be matched to ALISTER's stability database — e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-refmet-analyte-class-mapping))
- **lipid-residue-extraction**: Use when you have a processed lipidomics table (one lipid identifier per row, one sample per column, e.g. from pandas.read_excel) and need to reprocess analyte annotations into per-sample fatty acid residue abundances rather than individual molecular lipid levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-residue-extraction))
- **lipid-retention-time-rule-application**: Use when you have candidate lipid annotations from spectral library matching (e.g., XCMS + CAMERA output) with MS/MS scores, and you need to reduce false positives and predict previously unannotated lipids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-retention-time-rule-application) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-retention-time-rule-implementation**: Use when you have annotated lipid identities paired with LC-MS retention times in one or more datasets and want to quantify conformity of the ECN/IUP/ESCN relative RT ordering rules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-retention-time-rule-implementation))
- **lipid-rt-ecn-regression-modeling**: Use when a lipid feature table with identifications, retention times, and fatty acyl compositions needs per-class RT plausibility checks or filtering of likely incorrect identifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-rt-ecn-regression-modeling))
- **lipid-sample-separation-visualization**: Use when after normalizing and log-transforming lipidomics intensity data in a LipidomicsExperiment object, use this skill when you need to assess whether your sample groups (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-sample-separation-visualization))
- **lipid-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a lipidomics dataset (Skyline export, numerical matrix, or Metabolomics Workbench file) has been differentially analyzed with lipidr and you need to know which lipid classes or chain characteristics are enriched in the regulated lipids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-set-enrichment-analysis--84e085d7592582bd))
- **lipid-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when after completing two-group or multi-group differential expression analysis on lipidomics data when you have computed log fold-change (logFC) values and want to identify which lipid classes (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-set-enrichment-analysis))
- **lipid-set-enrichment-analysis**: Use when you have a LipidSigR differential-expression output table of lipid species and want to test whether a priori-defined lipid sets (e.g., lipid classes like Cer, TAG, PC O-) show statistically significant, concordant differences between two biological states (e.g., phenotypes). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-set-enrichment-analysis--4729faa3c852d4a8))
- **lipid-shorthand-name-parsing**: Use when lipid identifiers arrive as heterogeneous shorthand names (LIPID MAPS, SwissLipids, or search-engine dialects from LipidXplorer, LDA, MS-DIAL) and must be converted to a canonical, machine-comparable structure before distance computation or structural comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-shorthand-name-parsing))
- **lipid-shorthand-nomenclature-tokenization**: Use when labeled lipid species strings (e.g. from HDF5 'lipid_species' attributes) must be decomposed into headgroup/fatty-acyl/adduct tokens for seq2seq training, per-component accuracy scoring, or token-level prediction comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-shorthand-nomenclature-tokenization))
- **lipid-shorthand-notation-parsing**: Use when an uploaded lipidomic matrix or lipid-name list contains vendor shorthand (e.g., PE(P-34:1), sphingoid bases like d16:1, placeholders like 0:0) that must be normalized before LipidOne 2.0 analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-shorthand-notation-parsing))
- **lipid-shorthand-token-reassembly**: Use when running LipiDetective prediction on MS2 spectra and you have raw decoded token sequences (headgroup, fatty-acid chain, adduct, end-of-sequence tokens from the 1,032-token vocabulary) that must be converted to lipid shorthand notation strings with confidence values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-shorthand-token-reassembly))
- **lipid-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have two or more lipid structures (represented as molecular graphs or parsed lipid names) and need to quantify their structural dissimilarity for dendrogram construction, lipidome clustering, or identification of structurally similar lipids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-similarity-scoring))
- **lipid-species-abundance-counting**: Use when you have access to a lipidomics library repository (e.g., LipidMatch .csv files) and need to audit or report the total number of distinct lipid species and lipid-type categories present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-species-abundance-counting) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-species-annotation-assessment**: Use when after running MetaboAnnotatoR's annotateRC function when you need to (1) verify that the top-ranked annotation for a feature is correct, (2) understand what alternative lipid structures (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-species-annotation-assessment) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-species-annotation-parsing**: Use when raw lipid identification/quantification files from LipidSearch or LIQUID (or a Metabolomics Workbench experiment) must be converted into a structured table of lipid class, chain length, and double-bond strata before normalization or differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-species-annotation-parsing))
- **lipid-species-boundary-filtering**: Use when preprocessing vendor lipidomics output (e.g., LipidSearch or LIQUID tables) whose lipid species names must be validated against class-specific carbon-atom and double-bond limits before downstream normalization or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-species-boundary-filtering))
- **lipid-species-classification-mapping**: Use when you have a table of lipid species names or identifiers output from LipidSearch or LIQUID (with associated quantification data) and need to annotate each lipid with its standardized LIPID MAPS classification (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-species-classification-mapping))
- **lipid-species-enumeration-fatty-acyl-chain-space**: Use when a lipidomics identification module needs a comprehensive predicted lipid database spanning class and composition space beyond available experimental CCS/RT collections, especially for HILIC-IM-MS workflows. - **lipid-species-identification**: Use when you have centroid-mode LC–MS AIF chromatograms processed by xcms and RamClustR (or equivalent), a feature table with m/z and retention time coordinates, and you need to resolve individual lipid species identities with confidence scores and fragmentation evidence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-species-identification) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-species-parsing-lipid-maps-classification**: Use when a lipid abundance matrix lacks structured annotations (class, chain composition) and you need to decompose lipid species names before normalization, statistical analysis, or plotting. - **lipid-spectra-library-formatting**: Use when a completed Lipid Spectrum Generator run (lipids enumerated by class and fatty-acid composition, spectra per adduct) must be written out as a '.MSP' spectral library for downstream LC-MS/MS lipid identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-spectra-library-formatting))
- **lipid-spectral-data-export**: Use when after generating a complete lipid spectral library with adduct-specific fragmentation patterns and retention time metadata, when you need to deploy the library for targeted or data-dependent acquisition on specific mass spectrometry instruments—either Excalibur-controlled orbitrap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-spectral-data-export) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-spectral-library-generation**: Use when constructing a reference spectral library for lipid identification in liquid chromatography-tandem mass spectrometry (LC-MS/MS) and experimental library entries are missing or need custom lipid classes/adducts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-spectral-library-generation))
- **lipid-spectrum-generator-export-configuration**: Use when a downstream acquisition or analysis workflow (e.g. Skyline transition lists for targeted MS, or Excalibur precursor lists for Orbitrap DDA) needs lipid species generated by class, fatty-acid composition, and adduct in an importable file format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-spectrum-generator-export-configuration))
- **lipid-sphingolipid-fatty-acid-pathway-flux-analysis**: Use when comparing flux distributions between host-optimal and virus-optimal objectives in an integrated host–virus genome-scale metabolic model, especially to quantify pathway-level (lipid, sphingolipid, fatty acid) flux perturbations caused by maximal viral growth. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-sphingolipid-fatty-acid-pathway-flux-analysis))
- **lipid-structural-distance-matrix-computation**: Use when lipid species names have been parsed/standardized (e.g., via Goslin) and a pairwise structural distance/similarity matrix between all distinct lipid species is required for downstream PCA, lipidome comparison, or dendrogram clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-structural-distance-matrix-computation))
- **lipid-structural-feature-encoding**: Use when preparing structured inputs for the WMYn reverse-lipidomics model: lipid annotations (head group, chain length, degree of unsaturation per fatty acyl chain) must be converted into the feature representation the network consumes before MS2 fingerprint regeneration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-structural-feature-encoding--1eaf1e1a060a384b))
- **lipid-structural-feature-encoding**: Use when lipid species must be turned into the specialized 26-feature structural vector (lipid class, fatty acid modifier, sum composition) before fitting or applying LiPydomics CCS/HILIC retention time prediction models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-structural-feature-encoding--de259374cf74f101))
- **lipid-structural-level-mapping-lcl-lms-lbb**: Use when an uploaded lipidomic data matrix is annotated at molecular-species level (e.g., PC 34:1, TG 52:2 shorthand) and statistical or pathway analyses must be run per lipid class, per molecular species, or per acyl/alkyl building block. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-structural-level-mapping-lcl-lms-lbb))
- **lipid-structure-distance-computation-mcs-jaccard**: Use when pairwise structural distances or similarities between lipid species must be derived from their molecular structures (e.g., to build a structural space PCA model, an arc-shaped lipid ordering, or lipidome-level similarity), rather than from exact name matches or abundances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-structure-distance-computation-mcs-jaccard))
- **lipid-structure-maximum-common-subgraph-comparison**: Use when pairwise structural similarity between lipid species must be quantified from molecular graphs — e.g. building a lipid distance matrix, constructing a lipid space model, or testing whether counting double-bond positions changes the overlap between two fatty acids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-structure-maximum-common-subgraph-comparison))
- **lipid-structure-specification**: Use when you have identified lipid species unique to your sample type (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-structure-specification) · 2 copies)
- **lipid-subclass-recall-benchmarking**: Use when a published lipidomics dataset reports a known set of lipids (e.g. 2704 lipids in the mouse aging-associated lipidome atlas) and you must quantify how many are recovered by a spectral-library search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-subclass-recall-benchmarking))
- **lipid-transition-list-generation**: Use when designing targeted lipidomics assays in Skyline or stand-alone LipidCreator that need user-defined target lists and fragment libraries for PRM and MRM experiments across multiple lipid classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-transition-list-generation))
- **lipid-transition-response-regression**: Use when targeted SRM/PRM peak areas exported from Skyline for LipidCreator-computed transitions must be checked for linearity against analyte amount, e.g. verifying PUFA-PL ion signatures in a human platelet lipid matrix before releasing a transition list as an assay. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-transition-response-regression))
- **lipid-transition-spectral-library-export**: Use when a targeted lipidomics workflow needs a Skyline-consumable spectral library from computed lipid transitions — e.g., exporting CE-optimized platelet lipid-mediator transition lists as .blib for spectral matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-transition-spectral-library-export))
- **lipid-type-category-enumeration**: Use when when you have downloaded or cloned a lipidomics library repository (such as LipidMatch) and need to audit the breadth of lipid-type coverage to ensure the library meets minimum requirements for your analysis scope (e.g., ≥60 distinct lipid categories). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/lipid-type-category-enumeration) · 3 copies)
- **lipid-type-classification-tallying**: Use when you have a feature-level annotation workbook (e.g. Additional file 3, Table S4) from LipidMatch, MS-DIAL, and Greazy and need to count unique lipid ions, features, and lipid types for a given polarity such as positive-mode IE-ddMS2-top5 data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-type-classification-tallying))
- **lipid-type-composition-auditing**: Use when you need to quantify or verify the composition of an in silico lipid fragmentation library — e.g., counting lipid species, lipid types, fatty acid combinations, or class/adduct coverage from a library distribution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipid-type-composition-auditing))
- **lipidblast-in-silico-spectral-matching**: Use when annotating lipid-class features in untargeted AIF LC–MS/MS datasets where no experimental MS/MS spectra are available and in-silico coverage of lipids is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidblast-in-silico-spectral-matching))
- **lipidmaps-classification-mapping**: Use when importing lipid identification/quantification tables (LipidSearch or LIQUID outputs, or Metabolomics Workbench experiments) and each lipid species name must be decomposed into class, chain length, and double-bond strata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidmaps-classification-mapping))
- **lipidmaps-database-record-parsing**: Use when input records are lipid database entries with explicit double-bond positions on carbon chains and you need to extract per-chain double-bond position sets in both carbonyl-forward and methyl-end (ω) counting directions for pairwise comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidmaps-database-record-parsing))
- **lipidmaps-lmsd-mass-matching**: Use when a MultiABLER-processed LC-MS/MS feature table needs feature annotation, and lipid versus metabolite classification must be assigned by m/z matching against HMDB and LipidMaps reference mass databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidmaps-lmsd-mass-matching))
- **lipidmaps-reference-download**: Use when the goal is to quantify overlap between CMM 3.0's in-house oxidized lipid library and public lipid databases, or to establish whether CMM-exclusive oxidized lipid annotations are genuinely novel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidmaps-reference-download))
- **lipidome-distance-matrix-serialization**: Use when a multi-lipidome dataset must be reduced to a single pairwise distance matrix relating all lipidomes structurally, as the basis for hierarchical clustering or QC of large-scale lipidomics studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidome-distance-matrix-serialization))
- **lipidome-hausdorff-distance-computation**: Use when you have two or more lipidomes (e.g., csv/xlsx or mzTab-M tables with lipid species abundances) and need a global structural comparison relating all lipidomes, rather than only exact lipid-name matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidome-hausdorff-distance-computation))
- **lipidome-pca-projection-visualization**: Use when lipidome samples must be placed into a shared structural space: an n x n lipid-species distance matrix is available and per-lipidome coordinates plus reportable projection figures are the deliverable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidome-pca-projection-visualization))
- **lipidomic-data-matrix-reshaping**: Use when an uploaded lipidomic matrix is at molecular-species level (rows like PC, PE, TG species) but the target analysis is at the lipid class (LCL) or building block (LBB) structural level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomic-data-matrix-reshaping))
- **lipidomic-feature-group-statistics**: Use when a lipidomic data matrix with experimental group assignments must be tested per lipid feature (class, molecular species, or building-block level) to flag significant group differences with significance asterisks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomic-feature-group-statistics))
- **lipidomic-feature-table-preprocessing**: Use when raw lipidomic or metabolomic peak-intensity feature tables (e.g. MetaboLights S-BSST1038/S-BSST1039 exports) need filtering, imputation, log-transformation, and EigenMS normalization before downstream limma or ClassifyR analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomic-feature-table-preprocessing))

- **lipidomics-annotation-accounting**: Use when reproducing or auditing lipid annotation counts for an untargeted MS/MS study (e.g. ST002044) where library-match results must be merged with deep-learning fingerprint predictions under explicit Tanimoto and spectral-entropy cutoffs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomics-annotation-accounting))
- **lipidomics-annotation-appending**: Use when a feature table (m/z + retention time peaks) contains multiple co-eluting lipid candidates with similar m/z values, and you must rank candidate identifications and append final lipid annotations to the table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomics-annotation-appending))
- **lipidomics-annotation-table-summarization**: Use when comparing which lipid classes and adduct forms are enumerated or annotated across two or more in-silico lipid libraries, and you need to identify classes unique to a single library (e.g., OxPC only in MS-DIAL, CE only in CalicoLipids). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomics-annotation-table-summarization))
- **lipidomics-batch-effect-correction**: Use when a lipidomics abundance matrix (LipidSearch/LIQUID output or Metabolomics Workbench data) spans multiple acquisition batches and the user wants corrected values feeding the differential lipid abundance analysis (MA/volcano plots). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomics-batch-effect-correction))
- **lipidomics-ccs-correction-application**: Use when raw negative-mode TIMS CCS measurements from constituent datasets (e.g. Vasilopoulou et al., Tsugawa et al.) must be linearly calibrated toward DT reference CCS values and the post-correction MDRE reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/lipidomics-ccs-correction-application))

- **liquidacion-iva**: Skill FULL del módulo REFLEJO STATELESS `liquidacion-iva` de la vertical contabilidad (Enki). Calcula la LIQUIDACIÓN de IVA (devengado − soportado) por DEVENGADO, derivada del libro. **Los tipos son DATO — nunca cableados**; lo inclasificable se declara abierto. Los saldos se declaran o se piden best-effort a `mayor-balanza` (B). El lado (477 devengado / 472 soportado) se declara o se infiere por prefijo PGC. Escucha `contabilidad.asiento_asentado` y `contabilidad.ejercicio_cerrado`. Sin store propio. La op `calcular` es PREGUNTA → sin ui_handler. No recalcula: valida que no falten datos, pide los que falten con VACÍO FÁCTICO, y orquesta. Pregunta si la liquidación es extrajudicial o en sede judicial.
